Gradient for Q_d:
For d>0: Q_d = ∑_{i=0}^{n-d-1} h_i (1-h_{i+d}).
∂Q_d/∂h_i = (1-h_{i+d}) for i≤n-d-1 (as first)
∂Q_d/∂h_{i+d} = -h_i for i≤n-d-1 (as second), so for j>d, ∂ = -h_{j-d}. Thus array g length n:
g = zeros
g[:n-d] += (1 - h[d:])
g[d:] -= h[:-n+d]
For d<0: let e=-d: Q_{-e}=∑_{i=e}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-e}). By symmetry ∂h_i first =1-h_{i-e} for i≥e; ∂h_{i-e} second=-h_i. So g[e:]+=1-h[:-e]; g[:-e]-=h[e:].
For d=0: Q_0=∑ h_i(1-h_i), gradient 1-2h_i. But in binary objective maybe lag 0 not important; continuous important.

def smooth_draw(h,n,alpha):
 b=1-h; q=np.correlate(h,b,'full')
 qmax=q.max(); shifted=alpha*(q-qmax); shifted-=shifted.max() maybe; w=np.exp(shifted); w/=w.sum()
 F=qmax + np.log(w.sum())/alpha? Wait after shift: F = qmax + (smax + log(sum(exp(shift-sm))))/alpha. But if smax=0 after subtract max, log(sum(exp(shift-shiftmax)))? Since shift = alpha(q-qmax) already max 0. Then F = qmax + log(sum(exp(shift)))/alpha. If shift max 0, good.
 # gradient:
 g=np.zeros(n)
 d_vals = np.arange(-(n-1), n) # p=n-1-d
 for idx, p in enumerate(large where weight?): if w small skip? But all can contribute; to save, include weights >1e-4 or top K. If all active smooth weights, many nonzero. With large alpha, top few. We can just loop all 799 (fine) but skip weights <1e-7.
 For each d, wk:
   if d>0: g[:n-d] += wk*(1-h[d:]); g[d:] -= wk*h[:-n+d]
   elif d<0: e=-d; g[e:] += wk*(1-h[:-e]); g[:-e] -= wk*h[e:]
   else: g += wk*(1-2*h)
 return F, g, q

def binary_improve(h, n, deadline, max_iters):
 h=to_binary(h)
 b=1-h
 c=correlate reverse
 M=float(c.max())
 ones=where(h==1), zeros=...
 while time<deadline and iterations:
   tol=1e-12
   active_t = np.where(c >= M - 1 - 1e-9)[0]
   K=len(active_t)
   if K==0? M maybe no? handle.
   A = np.empty((n,K)); B = ...
   for k,t in enumerate(active_t): compute.
   # To find best pair, we can loop over zeros or choose smaller group. z_count=max? n/2.
   best_score=M; best_i=-1; best_j=-1
   # We want minimize new max among active. score_j = max_d active (c_active + B[j,d] + A[i,d])
   c_a = c[active_t]
   # If K small, broadcasting mat = B.T (K,m) + c_a[:,None] + A_i[:,None]
   # memory K*m small. Use float. For each i.
   for i in zeros:
       tmp = B[ones].T? If B precomputed all positions, B_ones = B[ones]. Need avoid copy each loop? B array n x K, B_ones = B[ones] copy once per state. A_i = A[i]
       if K == 1:
          scores = c_a[0] + B_ones[0] + A_i[0]
       else:
          scores = (B_ones + c_a[:,None] + A_i[:,None]).max(axis=0) # B_ones shape m,K. Wait c_a (K) + A_i (K) gives vector, need broadcast K,m: (c_a + A_i)[:,None] + B_ones.T? Let's get shapes: B_ones (m,K). B_ones.T (K,m). c_a+A_i shape K. (c_a+A_i)[:,None] + B_ones.T -> K,m. max(axis=0) -> m.
       # But also need ensure inactive not exceed? We'll verify.
       idx_min = argmin(scores)
       sc = scores[idx_min]
       if sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_i=i; best_j=ones[idx_min]
   if best_i<0 or best_score >= M - tol: maybe try random swaps/anneal or break.
   # verify full:
   delta=swap_full(best_i,best_j,h,n,b)
   nc = (c+delta).max()
   if nc < M - tol:
      update h,b,c,M, ones/zeros arrays.
      iterations++
      record best
   else:
      # maybe active too optimistic? If no improvement, break or do anneal.

Algorithm continuous_refine(h,n,deadline):
- Start h in [0,1], sum target.
- Use smooth F = logsumexp(alpha q)/alpha and gradient all lags (or top K? maybe all because n=400 feasible).
- Use adaptive line search with curvature
- Periodically recompute best max (full q max) and save.
- Use multiple alphas increasing.
- Use noise restarts.

Projected gradient step:
def pg_line_search(h,g,F, target, max_iter=20, line_max=0.5):
 step=line_max
 while:
  xm=project(h - step*g)
  F2 = F_smooth(xm) (recompute all, cost)
  if F2 < F - 1e-10: accept return xm,F2,step
  step*=0.5
  if step<1e-6: break
 # If no descent, try smaller? If fails, scale gradient by 1e-3? Maybe due nonconvex or projection; return current, F, fail.
But recomputing F each step heavy. Number iterations maybe 500. Acceptable? Each F compute np.correlate only O(C) no gradient; line search maybe few. Good.

We can avoid full line search by using fixed BB step update from previous F/h/g. Simpler: Use Barzilai-Borwein with projection:
Given h_k, g_k. If previous h_prev, g_prev: y=h_k-h_prev, s=g_k-g_prev. gamma = y^2/s^2 (BB2) or g^2/y^2. step=clip(gamma, 0.001, 5). Then line search. This often fast.

But first-order projected gradient with backtracking may be enough if time large. Use adaptive alpha.

continuous_optimize(h,n,deadline, seed):
 best_h = project(h)
 best_c = eval(best_h)
 target=n/2
 # alpha schedule maybe [10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
 for alpha in schedule:
   h_local=best_h.copy()
   F,g=smooth_obj_grad(h_local,n,alpha)
   step=1.0? Need maybe based on previous step.
   for it in range(max_iters e.g. 800 per alpha):
     if time>deadline: break
     # If gradient norm small break
     gn = np.linalg.norm(g)
     if gn < 1e-6: break
     # BB step? simple step with reduction if failed.
     accepted=False
     for ls in range(12):
       x = project_sum(h_local - step*g, target)
       x = np.clip? project does
       F2=smooth_value(x,n,alpha)
       # also compute full max to avoid? Use F2 only.
       if F2 < F - 1e-12:
          h_local=x; F=F2; accepted=True; break
       step *= 0.5
     if not accepted:
       # reduce alpha? or random jitter
       # Try smaller step with CAD? If no descent, maybe g zero due plateau; break
       step *= 0.1
       if step < 1e-4: break
     # update
     F,g=smooth_obj_grad(h_local,n,alpha)
     c=eval(h_local)
     if c < best_c - tol:
       best_c=c; best_h=h_local.copy()
       # reset step? increase maybe
       step*=1.1
     else:
       step*=0.9
   # continue next alpha from best_h (not local if local not improve)
   h_local=best_h.copy()
 return best_h,best_c

def coord_feasible(c, f, a, t, center, tol):
 low = -a
 high = 1.0 - a
 # vectorized update affine constraints
 # f size L. Need split positive/negative.
 pos = f > 1e-12
 neg = f < -1e-12
 # We can avoid allocate masks each time by precomputing? f changes per coordinate. Use masking.
 if np.any(pos): high = min(high, np.min((t - c[pos]) / f[pos]))
 if np.any(neg): low = max(low, np.max((t - c[neg]) / f[neg]))
 if low > high + 1e-12: return False
 # zero f constraints: if np.any(~(pos|neg)) and np.max(c[zero]) > t + tol -> false.

 else:
    return True

def feasible_coord(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max, c0, f0, dl, du, t):
    low=dl; high=du
    if c_pos.size:
       # need min ratio. If any t<c_pos? ratio negative; min handles.
       high = min(high, float(np.min((t - c_pos)/f_pos)))
    if c_neg.size:
       low = max(low, float(np.max((t - c_neg)/f_neg)))
       # f_neg negative, ratios maybe.
    if low > high + 1e-12: return False
    if zero_max > t + 1e-10: return False
    D = f0*f0 - 4*(t - c0)
    if D >= 0:
       r1 = (f0 - np.sqrt(D))/2; r2 = (f0 + np.sqrt(D))/2
       if low <= r1 or high >= r2: return True
    return True

def coordinate_polish(h,n, deadline, max_sweeps=200, its_per_coord=45):
 h=project_sum(h,n/2)
 c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
 M=c.max()
 best_h,best_c
 for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
   if time>deadline break
   order = list(range(n)) maybe random? deterministic or random.
   improved=False
   for i in order:
      if time>deadline break
      a=h[i]
      f=compute_coord_coeffs(h,i,n)
      delta, t_opt = coord_find_delta(c,f,a,n-1,its=45)
      if abs(delta)>1e-8:
         h[i]=a+delta
         c += delta*f
         c[center] -= delta*delta
         # due clip
         c = np.maximum(c,0)? Correlation nonnegative; numerical negative small. Could clip c to 0? But if clip, drift. Maybe c = np.clip(c,0,None) if min < -1e-8.
         # Evaluate new max exact
         newM = float(c.max())
         if newM < M - 1e-7:
            improved=True
         M = max(M, newM) # should not increase; but due numerical use newM.
   # recompute c to prevent drift, update M
   c_np=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   if np.max(np.abs(c_np-c)) > 1e-4: c=c_np
   else c = 0.5*(c+c_np)? maybe just recompute each sweep.
   M = c.max()
   cval = M*2/n
   if cval < best_c: best...
   if not improved and M stable break
 return best

Detailed coordinate_optimize:
def coordinate_optimize(h, n, deadline, max_sweeps=200, coord_iters=45, swap_order=True):
 target=n/2
 h=project_sum(h,target)
 c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').astype(float)
 M=float(c.max())
 best_c=M*2.0/n
 best_h=h.copy()
 center=n-1
 # maybe random generator inside? top-level uses np.random? Use local RNG.
 rng=np.random.RandomState(123)
 for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
   if time.time()>deadline: break
   # choose order: sorted random
   order=np.random.permutation(n) if sweep%2 else np.arange(n)
   improved=False
   # To avoid stale c, recompute at start sweep already.
   for i in order:
     if time.time()>deadline: break
     a=float(h[i])
     # skip if very close to bound? If a<=1e-9 or >=1-1e-9, still can move? yes if a=0 can move up? For continuous, moving 0 small maybe q0 increases; but could be useful. Allow.
     f = coord_coeffs(h, i, n)
     delta, tout = coord_find_delta(c, f, a, center, coord_iters)
     if abs(delta)>1e-8:
        # tentative
        c_old = c.copy()
        a_old = a
        h[i] = a + delta
        c += delta * f
        c[center] -= delta*delta
        # guard
        if np.any(c < -1e-6): c = np.clip(c,0.0,None)
        newM = float(c.max())
        if newM <= M + 1e-7:
           M = max(M, newM)  # should not exceed
           if newM < M - 1e-8? Wait M variable old; we need store oldM.
        else:
           c = c_old
           h[i] = a_old
        # for best update
        newC = M*2.0/n? Actually if M not decreased, okay. If decreased:
        if newM < best_c/dx - 1e-12: best...
   # after sweep recompute c
   c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').astype(float)
   M = float(c.max())
   cval = M*2.0/n
   if cval < best_c:
      best_c=cval; best_h=h.copy()
   # stagnation: if no coordinate decreased more than 1e-6? We can track oldM.
   if M >= previousM - 1e-7: maybe break after few.
   previousM=M
 return best_h,best_c

coord_find_delta implementation no closures:
def coord_find_delta(c, f, a, center, iters=45):
 hi = float(c.max())  # current M maybe pass to avoid recompute
 lo = 0.0
 # extract
 pos_mask = f > 1e-12
 pos_mask[center] = False
 neg_mask = f < -1e-12
 neg_mask[center] = False
 zero_mask = (f >= -1e-12) & (f <= 1e-12)
 zero_mask[center] = False
 c_pos = c[pos_mask]; f_pos=f[pos_mask]
 c_neg = c[neg_mask]; f_neg=f[neg_mask]
 zero_max = float(c[zero_mask].max()) if zero_mask.any() else -1e300
 c0 = float(c[center]); f0=float(f[center])
 dl = -a; du = 1.0 - a
 for _ in range(iters):
    mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    if coord_feasible(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max, c0, f0, dl, du, mid):
       hi = mid
    else:
       lo = mid
 # choose delta at hi with slack
 delta = coord_choose_delta(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max, c0, f0, dl, du, hi + 1e-8)
 return delta, hi

coord_feasible function:
def coord_feasible(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max, c0, f0, dl, du, t):
    low=dl; high=du
    if c_pos.size:
       r = float(np.min((t - c_pos)/f_pos))
       if r < high: high = r
    if c_neg.size:
       r = float(np.max((t - c_neg)/f_neg))
       if r > low: low = r
    if low > high + 1e-11: return False
    if zero_max > t + 1e-9: return False
    # Quadratic center constraint
    D = f0*f0 - 4.0*(t - c0)
    if D >= 0.0:
       sqrtD = np.sqrt(D)
       r1 = 0.5*(f0 - sqrtD)
       r2 = 0.5*(f0 + sqrtD)
       return (low <= r1 + 1e-12) or (high >= r2 - 1e-12)
    return True

For f negative, (t-c_neg)/f_neg: since f_neg<0, inequality t ≥ c_neg + f_neg δ -> f_neg δ ≥ t - c_neg -> divide by negative flips: δ ≤ (t-c_neg)/f_neg? Wait rederive.
For f<0, c + f δ ≤ t. Since f negative, f δ ≥ t - c (multiply by -1 flips). Divide by f (negative): δ ≤ (t-c)/f? Example c=5,f=-1,t=4: inequality 5-δ≤4 -> δ≥1. (t-c)/f =(-1)/(-1)=1; δ≤1? wrong. Let's do algebra: c + fδ ≤ t => fδ ≤ t-c. If f<0, divide yields δ ≥ (t-c)/f. Because dividing by negative flips: δ ≥ (t-c)/f. Example (-1)δ ≤ -1 => δ ≥1. Formula (t-c)/f = (-1)/(-1)=1. Good lower bound. So for f_neg, low = max(low, (t-c)/f). That matches previous (np.max ratios). For f>0, δ ≤ (t-c)/f, high min. Good.

For f=0, c≤t.

choose_delta:
def coord_choose_delta(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max, c0, f0, dl, du, t):
    low=dl; high=du
    if c_pos.size: high=min(high,float(np.min((t-c_pos)/f_pos)))
    if c_neg.size: low=max(low,float(np.max((t-c_neg)/f_neg)))
    if low > high + 1e-10:
       # fallback midpoint
       return 0.0
    if zero_max > t + 1e-7:
       return 0.0
    D=f0*f0 -4*(t-c0)
    cands=[]
    if D >=0:
       r1=(f0-np.sqrt(D))/2; r2=(f0+np.sqrt(D))/2
       # left interval [low, min(high,r1)]
       hi_left = min(high, r1)
       if low <= hi_left + 1e-12:
          left = np.clip(0.0, low, hi_left)
          cands.append(left)
       lo_right = max(low, r2)
       if lo_right <= high + 1e-12:
          right = np.clip(0.0, lo_right, high)
          cands.append(right)
    else:
       # whole interval
       cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0, low, high)))
    if not cands:
       return 0.0
    # choose candidate with smallest abs (nearest current)
    idx = int(np.argmin(np.abs(np.array(cands))))
    d = float(cands[idx])
    # margin
    if d < dl + 1e-9: d = max(dl, 0.5*(dl+du) if low? maybe) simpler clip
    d = float(np.clip(d, dl, du))
    # To avoid zero when t improvement? If abs small but expected improvement? We can return d if >1e-10 else maybe endpoint? If d=0 and feasible at t below current M? Could happen if left/right both contain 0 and 0 feasible -> current max <=t, so no improvement. Return 0 ok.
    if abs(d) < 1e-10:
        # if exact zero, maybe choose a small feasible step to perturb? But if 0 feasible, no immediate need. Could choose 0.
        return d
    return d

G_N=0; G_ALPHA=1.0
def sl_objective(x):
    n=G_N; alpha=G_ALPHA
    q=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    mu=q.max(); sh=alpha*(q-mu); sh-=sh.max(); e=np.exp(sh); return float(mu + np.log(e.sum())/alpha)

def project_sum(x,target):
    target=float(target)
    x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    # bisection
    lo=float(np.min(x)-1.0); hi=float(np.max(x)+1.0)
    # Need sum(clip(x-lam))=target. If x already close, can simple.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=(np.clip(x-mid,0,1).sum())
       if s > target: # decreasing mid increases sum? x-mid: if mid smaller, sum larger. To reduce sum, increase mid.
           lo=mid
       else:
           hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)/2
    y=np.clip(x-lam,0,1)
    # adjust tiny diff
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # distribute to non-boundary
       mask=(y>1e-12)&(y<1-1e-12)
       if mask.any(): y[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
       else:
          # sprinkle by order? simplify proportional
          y += diff/y.sum() if y.sum()>0 else diff/diff? 
    return np.clip(y,0,1)

Let's write coordinate_optimize carefully:
```
def coordinate_optimize(h,n,deadline,coord_iters=40,max_sweeps=100):
    target=0.5*n
    if n<=1: ...
    h=project_sum(h,target)
    center=n-1
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')[::-1].astype(float)
    c=np.clip(c,0.0,None)
    M=float(c.max())
    dx=2.0/n
    best_c=M*dx
    best_h=h.copy()
    old_M=M
    stall=0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(0)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       pre_M=M
       order=np.arange(n) if sweep%3==0 else rng.permutation(n)
       for i in order:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          a=float(h[i])
          # skip if at boundaries and no fraction? no
          f=coord_coeffs(h,i,n)
          delta,tout=coord_find_delta(c,f,a,center,coord_iters)
          if abs(delta)>1e-8:
             new_c = c + delta*f
             new_c[center] -= delta*delta
             # numerical nonneg
             if new_c.min() < -1e-7:
                new_c = np.clip(new_c,0.0,None)
             newM=float(new_c.max())
             if newM <= M + 1e-7:
                h[i]=a+delta
                c=new_c
                if newM < M - 1e-12:
                    M=newM
                    if M*dx < best_c:
                        best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
                # else equal
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')[::-1].astype(float)
       c=np.clip(c,0.0,None)
       M=float(c.max())
       if M*dx < best_c:
          best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
       if pre_M - M < 1e-8:
          stall += 1
       else:
          stall=0
       if stall>=4:
          break
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def coord_find_delta(c, f, a, center, iters, hi):
    hi = float(hi)
    lo = 0.0
    pos = f > 1e-12
    pos[center] = False
    neg = f < -1e-12
    neg[center] = False
    zero = ~(pos|neg)
    zero[center] = False
    if pos.any(): c_pos=c[pos]; f_pos=f[pos]
    else: c_pos=np.empty(0); f_pos=np.empty(0) ...
```

Now, SLSQP. Need ensure objective and gradient consistent. Use p-order q. Might not need reverse. Gradient loop:
```
def sl_grad(x):
 n=G_N; alpha=G_ALPHA
 b=1.0-x
 q=np.correlate(x,b,'full')
 mu=q.max()
 sh=alpha*(q-mu)
 sh-=sh.max()
 w=np.exp(sh)
 wsum=w.sum()
 if wsum<=0: return np.zeros(n)
 w /= wsum
 g=np.zeros(n)
 center=n-1
 # loop over nonzero
 idxs=np.where(w>1e-10)[0]
 for p in idxs:
   wk=w[p]
   d=center-p
   if d>0:
     m=n-d
     g[:m] += wk*(1.0-x[d:])
     g[d:] -= wk*x[:m]
   elif d<0:
     e=-d
     g[e:] += wk*(1.0-x[:-e])
     g[:n-e] -= wk*x[e:]
   else:
     g += wk*(1.0-2.0*x)
 return g
```
If alpha very large, w for one lag 1. Good.


Let's draft code precisely.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

G_N = 0
G_ALPHA = 1.0

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_sum(x, target):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    target = float(target)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    if abs(x.sum() - target) < 1e-14:
        return x
    lo = float(x.min()) - 0.2 # since [0,1], lambda can range; use min-1, max+1
    hi = float(x.max()) + 0.2 # Need enough to achieve sum 0 or n. If x=0,target>0, lambda negative <0; min-1=-1 works. If x=1 target< n, lambda >1; max+1=2. Use +-1.
```
Use lo = x.min()-1.0, hi=x.max()+1.0 safe.
```
    for _ in range(80):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = np.clip(x-mid,0,1).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo=mid
        else:
            hi=mid
    lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
    y=np.clip(x-lam,0,1)
    # final correction
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if diff != 0:
        room_up = (y < 1.0); room_up_abs = room_up.sum()
        room_down = (y > 0.0); ...
        if diff >0:
           if room_up_abs: y[room_up] += diff/room_up_abs
        else:
           if room_down_abs: y[room_down] += diff/room_down_abs
    return np.clip(y,0,1)
```
But if adding diff/room_up may push above 1 if diff large. But after bisection diff tiny <1e-10, okay. Use 80 iters to minimize diff. Could skip correction or do safe:
```
    y = np.clip(x - lam, 0,1)
    # tiny adjustment through non-boundaries
    diff = target - y.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       mask = (y>1e-8)&(y<1-1e-8)
       if mask.any():
          y[mask] += diff / mask.sum()

```
This may still sum off by tiny. Good.

`coord_coeffs`:
```
def coord_coeffs(h,i,n):
    L=2*n-1
    center=n-1
    f=np.zeros(L)
    b=1.0-h
    f[center-i:center-i+n] += b
    f[center+i-n+1:center+i+1] += -h[::-1]
    return f
```

`coord_feasible`:
Use arrays c_pos,f_pos,c_neg,f_neg,zero_max,c0,f0,dl,du,t. Avoid `c_pos.size` if python list? arrays.
```
def coord_feasible(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max, c0, f0, dl, du, t):
    low=dl; high=du
    if c_pos.size:
        r = float(np.min((t - c_pos) / f_pos))
        if r < high: high = r
    if c_neg.size:
        r = float(np.max((t - c_neg) / f_neg))
        if r > low: low = r
    if low > high + 1e-11:
        return False
    if zero_max > t + 1e-9:
        return False
    D = f0*f0 - 4.0*(t - c0)
    if D >= 0.0:
        sqrtD = np.sqrt(D)
        r1 = 0.5*(f0 - sqrtD)
        r2 = 0.5*(f0 + sqrtD)
        return (low <= r1 + 1e-12) or (high >= r2 - 1e-12)
    return True
```
What if `t` below zero, zero_max negative? zero_max could be 0 or positive. OK.

`coord_choose_delta`:
```
def coord_choose_delta(c_pos,f_pos,c_neg,f_neg,zero_max,c0,f0,dl,du,t):
    low=dl; high=du
    if c_pos.size:
        r=float(np.min((t-c_pos)/f_pos)); high=min(high,r)
    if c_neg.size:
        r=float(np.max((t-c_neg)/f_neg)); low=max(low,r)
    if low > high + 1e-10:
       return 0.0
    if zero_max > t + 1e-7:
       return 0.0
    D=f0*f0-4.0*(t-c0)
    cands=[]
    if D >=0:
       r1=0.5*(f0-np.sqrt(D)); r2=0.5*(f0+np.sqrt(D))
       if low <= r1 + 1e-12:
           hi_l = min(high, r1)
           left = float(np.clip(0.0, low, hi_l))
           cands.append(left)
       if r2 <= high + 1e-12:
           lo_r = max(low, r2)
           right = float(np.clip(0.0, lo_r, high))
           cands.append(right)
    else:
       cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0, low, high)))
    if not cands: return 0.0
    # if multiple, choose smaller abs; tie choose? use argmin of abs then maybe smaller step.
    best = cands[0]
    best_abs = abs(best)
    for val in cands[1:]:
       av=abs(val)
       if av < best_abs - 1e-12:
          best=val; best_abs=av
    d=float(np.clip(best, dl, du))
    # add tiny epsilon if at bound? not necessary
    return d
```
If cands left interval contains 0, left=0, choose 0. Good.

`coord_find_delta`:
```
def coord_find_delta(c,f,a,center,iters,hi):
    lo=0.0
    hi=float(hi)
    pos=f>1e-12
    pos[center]=False
    neg=f<-1e-12
    neg[center]=False
    zero=~(pos|neg)
    zero[center]=False
    c_pos=c[pos]; f_pos=f[pos]
    c_neg=c[neg]; f_neg=f[neg]
    zero_max = float(c[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e300
    c0=float(c[center]); f0=float(f[center])
    dl=-float(a); du=1.0-float(a)
    # ensure hi at least actual? if hi<lo? no
    for _ in range(iters):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        if coord_feasible(c_pos,f_pos,c_neg,f_neg,zero_max,c0,f0,dl,du,mid):
            hi=mid
        else:
            lo=mid
    d=coord_choose_delta(c_pos,f_pos,c_neg,f_neg,zero_max,c0,f0,dl,du,hi+1e-8)
    return d, hi
```
Potential performance: `zero=~(pos|neg)` allocates. okay.

But `f[center]` after setting pos/neg false but f unchanged. Good.

Coordinate optimize uses `order = np.arange(n)` first sweep, then random? If use `rng.permutation(n)` every sweep, random. It modifies h in random order; fine. But after SLSQP maybe h not binary. We'll use deterministic first, then random.

```
def coordinate_optimize(h,n,deadline,coord_iters=40,max_sweeps=1000):
    if n < 2: return project_sum(h,n/2), compute_c5(h)
    h=project_sum(h,0.5*n)
    center=n-1; dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')[::-1].astype(float)
    c=np.clip(c,0.0,None)
    M=float(c.max())
    best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
    stale=0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(12345)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       pre=M
       if sweep==0:
          order=np.arange(n)
       else:
          order=rng.permutation(n)
       for i in order:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          a=float(h[i])
          f=coord_coeffs(h,i,n)
          delta,tout=coord_find_delta(c,f,a,center,coord_iters,M)
          if abs(delta)>1e-8:
             new_c=c+delta*f
             new_c[center]-=delta*delta
             if new_c.min() < -1e-6:
                new_c=np.clip(new_c,0.0,None)
             newM=float(new_c.max())
             if newM <= M + 1e-7:
                h[i]=a+delta
                c=new_c
                if newM < M - 1e-12:
                    M=newM
                    if M*dx < best_c:
                       best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')[::-1].astype(float)
       c=np.clip(c,0.0,None)
       M=float(c.max())
       if M*dx < best_c:
          best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
       if pre - M < 1e-8:
          stale +=1
       else:
          stale=0
       if stale>=5:
          break
    best_h=project_sum(best_h,0.5*n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h,best_c
```
Need be careful: In inner loop, after updating h[i], future coord_coeffs use updated h, and c updated. Good. `f` computed before update; after update h changes. Future coordinates recompute f. Good.
`new_c.min()` each accepted coordinate O(L). Could be okay. To save, remove if no negative? But keep.
`if pre - M < 1e-8` uses pre sweep M before recompute? We set pre=M before sweep. If M only decreased by equal moves no. good.
Stale after a sweep with no M decrease. But coordinate might need several equal-move sweeps to restructure? If no M decrease in a full sweep, further sweeps likely no. Break after 5. Maybe too aggressive? Equal moves could set up future decrease; but if full sweep no M decrease, maybe current coordinate optimum. Since we allow M-equal moves, the next random order might find. 5 sweeps enough.

Potential issue: `coord_find_delta` uses hi=M. During sweep, if M decreased, pass current M. Good.

Now, SLSQP. Let's implement `sl_obj`, `sl_grad`. Need if `G_ALPHA` very large, `alpha*(q-mu)` underflows to 0 except max; exp sum ~ count of max. F=mu + log(count)/alpha. Good.
If alpha small, okay.
```
def sl_obj(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n=G_N; alpha=G_ALPHA
    q=np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, 'full')
    mu=float(q.max())
    sh=alpha*(q-mu)
    sh-=float(sh.max())
    e=np.exp(sh)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s <=0: return mu
    return float(mu + np.log(s)/alpha)
```
If alpha = 1e6, sh max 0, min maybe -1e6? exp underflow, s~1. fine.
`sl_grad`:
```
def sl_grad(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    n=G_N; alpha=G_ALPHA; center=n-1
    q=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    mu=q.max(); sh=alpha*(q-mu); sh-=sh.max()
    e=np.exp(sh); s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    w=e/s
    g=np.zeros(n)
    # threshold
    thr=1e-12
    idxs=np.nonzero(w>thr)[0]
    for p in idxs:
       wk=float(w[p]); d=center-p
       if d>0:
          m=n-d
          g[:m] += wk*(1.0-x[d:])
          g[d:] -= wk*x[:m]
       elif d<0:
          e_=-d
          g[e_:] += wk*(1.0-x[:-e_])
          g[:n-e_] -= wk*x[e_:]
       else:
          g += wk*(1.0-2.0*x)
    return g
```
Could be slow if many w>thr; for alpha small many, but n=400. Maybe use top K? For alpha small, w spread among 800; 800 loop. SLSQP calls maybe many; okay. Could reduce with alpha not too small (min 5). For alpha 5, weights spread; loop all. acceptable.
`sl_cons`:
```
def sl_cons(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*G_N)
```
`slsqp_smooth`:
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    global G_N,G_ALPHA
    G_N=int(n); G_ALPHA=float(alpha)
    target=0.5*n
    h=project_sum(h,target)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':sl_cons}]
    try:
       res=minimize(sl_obj,h,jac=sl_grad,bounds=bounds,constraints=constraints,
                    method='SLSQP',options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12,'disp':False})
       x=res.x
    except Exception:
       x=h
    x=project_sum(x,target)
    return x
```
Potential SLSQP with `ftol=1e-12` may run maxiter. Use `ftol=1e-10` for speed. objective scale ~100, ftol 1e-10 okay. For high precision maybe 1e-12. Use 1e-11.

SLSQP can call sl_grad with x not in bounds; if x slightly negative, q maybe? fine. If alpha global not set? set.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):

    # If n > 800, downsample to 600?

       n=nd

    # phase2 SLSQP if time and n<=600
    p2=t0+0.72*budget_s

        alphas=[5.0,15.0,60.0,250.0,1000.0,4000.0,15000.0]
```

Implement f_all construction:
```
def build_f_all(h,n):
  L=2*n-1; center=n-1; F=np.zeros((n,L))
  b=1-h
  hrev=h[::-1]
  for p in range(n):
     F[p, center-p:center-p+n] += b
     F[p, center+p-n+1:center+p+1] += -hrev
  return F
```
This loops n, each two slice assignments length n -> O(n^2)=160k for n=400, fine. Memory 400*799*8=2.56MB for n=400; n=800: 800*1599*8=10.2MB, fine.

Update f_all after changing h_i/h_j from a to new, b old to new. We can update contributions of changed variable k with old h_k to new h_k. f_p is sum of b at p+d and -h at p-d. The value b_k =1-h_k appears in first term for all p where p+d=k => d=k-p, index idx1=center+d=center+k-p. The value -h_k appears in second term for all p where p-d=k => d=p-k, idx2=center+p-k. For each p 0..n-1:
 idx1 = center+k-p; valid if 0<=idx1<L => p between k-(n-1) and k+n-1, but p always 0..n-1 so always? d=k-p ranges -(n-1)..n-1 yes idx in 0..L-1. So for all p, first term at idx1 valid. The old contribution to f_p[idx1] was b_k_old=1-h_k_old; new b_k_new. Delta_b = (1-new) - (1-old) = old-new = -delta_k (where delta_k = new-old). So add -delta_k to f_all[p, idx1].
Second term idx2=center+k? Wait p-d=k => d=p-k, idx=center+p-k. For all p, valid. Old contribution -h_k_old; new -h_k_new; delta = -(new-old) = -delta_k. Add -delta_k to f_all[p, idx2]. Wait first delta: b=1-h; if h increases by delta, b decreases by delta: old b - new b? f contribution increases by new - old = (1-new)-(1-old)=old-new=-delta. So add -delta_k. Second: contribution -h; new-old = -delta. So add -delta_k. Interesting both -delta for h increase. Let's test n=2, h0 increase by δ. f_all for p=0 previously first at idx center+0? b0; second idx center+0? h0? For p=0,k=0: idx1=center+0=1, idx2=center+0=1 same (d=0); total change -2δ? Detailed pairwise exact feasibility with A coefficient.

General feasibility for linear constraints and one quadratic center:
q_d' = c_d + F_d δ for d≠0
q0' = c0 + F0 δ - A δ², A=2.
Bounds δ_l, δ_u.
Feasibility for t:
 affine bounds: F_d>0 -> δ ≤ (t-c_d)/F_d; F_d<0 -> δ ≥ (t-c_d)/F_d; F=0 -> c_d≤t.
Quadratic: c0 + F0 δ - A δ² ≤ t -> A δ² - F0 δ + (t - c0) ≥ 0 (since A>0). Discriminant D = F0² - 4A(t-c0). If D<0, always positive? For leading A>0 and negative discriminant, quadratic always positive (unless? yes >0), so q0<t all δ. If D>=0, roots r1=(F0 - sqrtD)/(2A), r2=(F0+sqrtD)/(2A); feasible outside [r1,r2].
Choose δ nearest 0 in feasible interval at t.

Implement:
```
def pair_feasible(c_pos,f_pos,c_neg,f_neg,zero_max,c0,F0,A,dl,du,t):
 low=dl; high=du
 if c_pos.size: high=min(high, min((t-c_pos)/f_pos))
 if c_neg.size: low=max(low, max((t-c_neg)/f_neg))
 if low>high+... return False
 if zero_max>t+... return False
 D=F0*F0 - 4*A*(t-c0)
 if D>=0:
   r1=(F0-np.sqrt(D))/(2*A); r2=...
   return low<=r1 or high>=r2
 return True
```
For single coordinate A=1, F0=f0. Good.

```
def pairwise_optimize(h,n,deadline, coord_iters=40, max_sweeps=200, K=30):
    if n<2: return...
    h=project_sum(h,0.5*n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')[::-1].astype(float); c=np.clip(c,0,None)
    M=float(c.max()); dx=2/n; best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
    f_all=build_f_all(h,n)
    center=n-1; stale=0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seedmaybe)
    for sweep:
       pre=M
       act = top K indices by c (d-order)
       f_act = f_all[:,act]  # n x K
       for i in order:
           a=h[i]
           # low/up arrays for all j
           low = np.maximum(-a, h - 1.0) # since j h -1
           up = np.minimum(1.0-a, h)
           # for j=i, set inf maybe later.
           Fmat = f_all[i][act, None] - f_act  # K x n
           # But f_act first column? okay.
           # We need exclude j=i and j where up<=low.
           # Compute c + delta contribution
           # low/up broadcast
           add = np.where(Fmat > 0.0, Fmat * low[None,:], Fmat * up[None,:])
           scores = (c_act[:,None] + add).max(axis=0)
           scores[i] = np.inf
           if up <= low: scores[...] inf
           j = int(np.argmin(scores))
           if not finite: break
           F = f_all[i] - f_all[j]
           delta,tout = pair_find_delta(c,F,a,float(h[j]),center,iters,M)
           if abs(delta)>1e-8:
              new_c = c + delta*F
              new_c[center] -= 2*delta*delta
              newM = float(new_c.max())
              if newM <= M + 1e-7:
                  # update h
                  h[i] = a + delta
                  h[j] = float(h[j]) - delta # careful h[j] changed if j==i no
                  # if j variable, update f_all for i delta, j -delta
                  update_f_all(f_all,h? or delta, i, delta, n)
                  update_f_all(f_all, j, -delta, n)
                  c = new_c
                  if newM < M -1e-12:
                     M=newM; update best; recompute act/f_act?

       recompute c f_all? To prevent drift, recompute c and f_all every sweep or every accepted. Build f_all each sweep O(n^2) okay. Do after sweep.
       if pre-M<1e-9 stale++; if stale>=5 break
    return best
```

```
def update_f_all(f_all, k, delta, n):
    center=n-1
    # delta is change h_k += delta
    # For all p, idx1=center+k-p, idx2=center+p-k, add -delta
```

Pseudo `pairwise_optimize`:
```
def pairwise_optimize(h,n,deadline,iters=40,max_sweeps=1000):
    if n<2: return project_sum(h,n/2), compute_c5(h)
    h=project_sum(h,0.5*n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')[::-1].astype(float)
    c=np.clip(c,0.0,None)
    M=float(c.max()); dx=2.0/n
    best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
    f_all=build_f_all(h,n)
    center=n-1
    K = 40 if n<=500 else 60
    stale=0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(9999)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        pre=M
        order=np.arange(n) if sweep%2==0 else rng.permutation(n)
        act = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
        f_act = f_all[:,act]
        c_act = c[act]
        accepts=0
        for i in order:
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            a=float(h[i])
            low = np.maximum(-a, h - 1.0)
            up = np.minimum(1.0-a, h)
            Fmat = f_all[i][act, None] - f_act
            add = np.where(Fmat > 0.0, Fmat * low[None,:], Fmat * up[None,:])
            scores = (c_act[:,None] + add).max(axis=0)
            scores[i]=np.inf
            # maybe mask up<=low+ tiny? not needed, but for safety
            bad = up <= low + 1e-12
            if bad.any(): scores[bad]=np.inf
            j=int(np.argmin(scores))
            if not np.isfinite(scores[j]): break
            bj=float(h[j])
            F = f_all[i] - f_all[j]
            delta,tout = pair_find_delta(c,F,a,bj,center,iters,M)
            if abs(delta)>1e-8:
                new_c = c + delta*F
                new_c[center] -= 2.0*delta*delta
                if new_c.min() < -1e-6:
                    new_c = np.clip(new_c,0.0,None)
                newM=float(new_c.max())
                if newM <= M + 1e-7:
                    h[i]=a+delta
                    h[j]=bj-delta
                    # update f_all
                    update_f_all(f_all,i,delta,n)
                    update_f_all(f_all,j,-delta,n)
                    c=new_c
                    if newM < M - 1e-12:
                       M=newM; accepts+=1
                       if M*dx < best_c: best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
                       act = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]; f_act=f_all[:,act]; c_act=c[act]
                    else:
                       # equal but f_all/c updated? If M equal, still update active? maybe not.
                       pass
        c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')[::-1].astype(float)
        c = np.clip(c,0.0,None)
        M = float(c.max())
        f_all = build_f_all(h,n)
        if M*dx < best_c: best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
        if pre - M < 1e-8:
           stale+=1
        else:
           stale=0
        if stale>=4:
           break
    best_h=project_sum(best_h,0.5*n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h,best_c
```

For one k, idx1 and idx2 arrays may have overlap with previous? For a single call, idx1 and idx2 are distinct except one index. But we do two separate calls: `f_all[np.arange(n), idx1] += -delta` and `... idx2 += -delta`. Advanced indexing with unique indices per call; if idx1 has duplicates? p unique; idx1 values unique (decreasing by1). Good. If idx1 and idx2 calls separate, if they overlap at p=k, total -2delta. Good.

`pair_find_delta`:
```
def pair_find_delta(c,F,a,b,center,iters,hi):
    dl = max(-a, b-1.0); du = min(1.0-a, b)
    if dl >= du: # if only zero
       return 0.0, float(hi)
    # if du very close dl, return diff/2?
    pos = F > 1e-12; pos[center]=False
    neg = F < -1e-12; neg[center]=False
    zero = ~(pos|neg); zero[center]=False
    c_pos=c[pos]; f_pos=F[pos]
    c_neg=c[neg]; f_neg=F[neg]
    zero_max = float(c[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e300
    c0=float(c[center]); F0=float(F[center]); A=2.0
    lo=0.0; hi=float(hi)
    for _ in range(iters):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if pair_feasible(... mid): hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    d=pair_choose_delta(... hi+1e-8)
    # ensure within dl,du
    return d, hi
```
But `pair_feasible` and `pair_choose_delta` accept A. Implement A param.

`pair_choose_delta`: For quadratic with A=2. Feasible intervals outside roots. Choose nearest 0. Also need ensure interval within [dl,du]. If D<0 entire interval. If D>=0: left [low, min(high,r1)], right [max(low,r2), high]. Choose nearest 0.
Use `np.clip(0.0, low, hi_l)` if low<=hi_l. If 0 not in interval, closest endpoint. Good.

Potential issue with `zero_max`: for F=0 lags other than center, c must ≤t. If c has tiny >t, infeasible. Good. For active score, F=0 lower bound c.

Now, SLSQP can still be used to refine multi-coordinate. Projected gradient maybe not needed. Pairwise exact likely strong.

But need verify pair update formula for q0: `new_c = c + delta*F; new_c[center] -= 2δ²`. F[center]=f_i0 - f_j0. For h_i delta +, h_j delta -. Old q0: a(1-a)+b(1-b). New: (a+δ)(1-a-δ)+(b-δ)(1-b+δ) = old + δ(1-2a) -δ² -δ(1-2b) -δ² = old + δ[(1-2a)-(1-2b)] -2δ². Yes.

For d≠0, if i/j contributions overlap? δF works. F includes f_i - f_j. If i and j same index for a given d? d≠0; if i+d = j-d perhaps terms involving same product? Still linear separately; no quadratic for d≠0? q_d product h_x b_{x+d}; changing two variables could create product between them? Wait if the two changed variables are the two factors of the same term? For a given d≠0, term h_p b_{p+d}. If we change h_i and h_j, and one is p, the other is p+d, the term's product changes bilinearly! Our δF formula assumes each q_d linear in each changed variable, but when both variables are in the same product, there is a cross term δ_i δ_j. For d≠0, if {i,j} = {p,p+d}, then changing both affects the same product h_p(1-h_{p+d}) with both. The change is not linear; there is δ_i δ_j. I missed! For coordinate single, no issue. Feasibility function signature:
```
def pair_feasible(c_pos,f_pos,c_neg,f_neg,zero_max,c0,F0,c1,F1,c2,F2,delta_l,delta_u,t):
   low=delta_l; high=delta_u
   if c_pos.size: high=min(high, min((t-c_pos)/f_pos))
   if c_neg.size: low=max(low, max((t-c_neg)/f_neg))
   if low > high+tol: return False
   if zero_max > t+tol: return False
   # convex1
   if not apply_convex(low,high,c1,F1,t): return False
   if not apply_convex(low,high,c2,F2,t): return False
   # concave center
   D=F0*F0 -8*(t-c0)
   if D>=0:
      r1=(F0-sqrt(D))/4; r2=(F0+sqrt(D))/4
      if not ((high <= r1+tol) or (low >= r2-tol)): return False
   # else concave always feasible
   return True
```
But `apply_convex` may need return updated low/high. Since Python function can't modify by reference; do inline in feasible and choose. Could define helper `convex_update(low, high, c, F, t)` returning (low,high,ok). Top-level helper.

```
def convex_update(low, high, c, F, t):
    D = F*F - 4.0*(c - t)
    if D < -1e-10: return low, high, False
    if D <= 1e-10:
       r = -0.5*F
       if r < low - 1e-10 or r > high + 1e-10: return low, high, False
       return r, r, True
    sqrtD=np.sqrt(D)
    r1=0.5*(F - sqrtD); r2=0.5*(F + sqrtD)
    low=max(low,r1); high=min(high,r2)
    if low>high+1e-10: return low,high,False
    return low,high,True
```

```
def pair_feasible(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max, c0, F0, c1, F1, c2, F2, dl, du, t):
    low=dl; high=du
    tol=1e-10
    if c_pos.size:
       r=float(np.min((t - c_pos)/f_pos))
       if r < high: high=r
    if c_neg.size:
       r=float(np.max((t - c_neg)/f_neg))
       if r > low: low=r
    if low > high + 1e-9: return False
    if zero_max > t + 1e-8: return False
    # convex 1
    D=F1*F1 - 4*(c1 - t)
    if D < -1e-9: return False
    if D <= 1e-11:
       r = -0.5*F1
       if r < low - 1e-9 or r > high + 1e-9: return False
       low=high=r
    else:
       s=np.sqrt(D); r1=0.5*(-F1 - s); r2=0.5*(-F1 + s)
       if r1 > low: low=r1
       if r2 < high: high=r2
       if low > high + 1e-9: return False
    # convex 2 similar
    ...
    # center
    D=F0*F0 - 8*(t - c0)
    if D >= 0:
       s=np.sqrt(D); r1=0.5*(F0 - s)/2? Wait formula (F0 - s)/4. Use r1=(F0-s)/4, r2=(F0+s)/4.
       if not ((high <= r1 + 1e-9) or (low >= r2 - 1e-9)):
          return False
    return True
```
For convex D slightly negative but within tol, we treat as root -F/2? If D<=1e-11. If D negative small, root -F/2; okay.

```
def pair_choose_delta(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max, c0, F0, c1, F1, c2, F2, dl, du, t):
   low=dl; high=du
   # same linear/convex updates, if infeasible return 0.0
   # after convex, interval [low,high]

         hi=min(high, r1)
         if low <= hi + tol: cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0, low, hi)))
      if high >= r2 - tol:
         lo=max(low, r2)
         if lo <= high + tol: cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0, lo, high)))
   else:
      cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0, low, high)))
   if not cands: return 0.0
   choose nearest 0
   return clip(best,dl,du)
```
Important: If after convex D=0 low=high=r, center condition handles: if r<=r1 or r>=r2. For left interval low<=r1 and hi=min(high,r1)=r; choose clip. Good.

Let's implement `pair_feasible` with repeated convex code. To keep lines short, define helper `apply_convex_interval(low, high, c, F, t)` returning `(low, high, ok)`. Top-level.

```
def convex_bounds(low, high, c, F, t):
    D = F*F - 4.0*(c - t)
    if D < -1e-10:
        return low, high, False
    if D <= 1e-11:
        r = -0.5*F
        if r < low - 1e-10 or r > high + 1e-10:
            return low, high, False
        return r, r, True
    s = np.sqrt(D)
    r1 = -0.5*(F + s)
    r2 = -0.5*(F - s)
    if r1 > low: low = r1
    if r2 < high: high = r2
    if low > high + 1e-10:
        return low, high, False
    return low, high, True
```
Check r1/r2: roots (-F ± s)/2. r1 = (-F - s)/2 = -0.5*(F+s). r2 = (-F + s)/2 = -0.5*(F-s). Good.

Then `pair_feasible` calls convex_bounds twice.

```
def pair_feasible(c_pos,f_pos,c_neg,f_neg,zero_max,c0,F0,c1,F1,c2,F2,dl,du,t):
    low=dl; high=du
    if c_pos.size:
       r=float(np.min((t-c_pos)/f_pos));
       if r<high: high=r
    if c_neg.size:
       r=float(np.max((t-c_neg)/f_neg));
       if r>low: low=r
    if low > high + 1e-9: return False
    if zero_max > t + 1e-8: return False
    low,high,ok = convex_bounds(low,high,c1,F1,t)
    if not ok: return False
    low,high,ok = convex_bounds(low,high,c2,F2,t)
    if not ok: return False
    D=F0*F0 - 8.0*(t-c0)
    if D >= 0.0:
       s=np.sqrt(D)
       r1=(F0-s)*0.25
       r2=(F0+s)*0.25
       if not (high <= r1 + 1e-9 or low >= r2 - 1e-9):
          return False
    return True
```

`pair_choose_delta`:
```
def pair_choose_delta(...):
    low=dl; high=du; same linear
    if low > high+1e-9: return 0
    if zero_max > t+1e-7: return 0
    low,high,ok=convex_bounds(...); if not ok return 0
    low,high,ok=convex_bounds(...); if not ok return 0
    D=...
    cands=[]
    if D >=0:
       s; r1;r2
       if low <= r1+1e-10:
           hi=min(high,r1)
           if low <= hi+1e-10: cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0,low,hi)))
       if high >= r2-1e-10:
           lo=max(low,r2)
           if lo <= high+1e-10: cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0,lo,high)))
    else:
       cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0,low,high)))
    if not cands: return 0
    best= cands[0]; besta=abs(best)
    for v in cands[1:]: ...
    return float(np.clip(best,dl,du))
```

```
def pair_find_delta(c,F,a,b,center,iters,hi,sp1,sp2):
    dl=max(-a, b-1.0); du=min(1.0-a, b)
    if du - dl <= 1e-13: return 0.0, float(hi)
    pos=F>1e-12; neg=F<-1e-12
    for idx in (center,sp1,sp2): pos[idx]=False; neg[idx]=False
    zero=~(pos|neg)
    for idx in (center,sp1,sp2): zero[idx]=False
    c_pos=c[pos]; f_pos=F[pos]; c_neg=c[neg]; f_neg=F[neg]
    zero_max = float(c[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e300
    c0=float(c[center]); F0=float(F[center]); c1=float(c[sp1]); F1=float(F[sp1]); c2=float(c[sp2]); F2=float(F[sp2])
    lo=0.0; hi=float(hi)
    for _ in range(iters):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if pair_feasible(...mid): hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    d=pair_choose_delta(... hi+1e-8)
    if d < dl: d=dl
    elif d > du: d=du
    return d, hi
```

Line by line:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

G_N = 0
G_ALPHA = 1.0

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')) * (2.0 / n))
```

`project_sum` final adjust:
```
def project_sum(x, target):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    target = float(target)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    if abs(x.sum() - target) < 1e-13:
        return x
    lo = float(x.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(x.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if float(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()) > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        mask = (y > 1e-9) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-9)
        if mask.any():
            y[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
    return np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
```
If diff positive and many mask, could push some above 1 if diff large; but bisection diff tiny. Good.

`build_f_all`:
```
def build_f_all(h,n):
    L=2*n-1; center=n-1
    F=np.zeros((n,L),dtype=float)
    b=1.0-h
    hr=h[::-1]
    for p in range(n):
        F[p, center-p:center-p+n] += b
        F[p, center+p-n+1:center+p+1] += -hr
    return F
```
For p integer, slices correct. If n=1, L=1, center=0. F[0,0:1]+=b; F[0,0:1]+= -hr. F[0]=1-2h. Pairwise n<2 returns early, but build maybe n=1. good.

`update_f_all`:
```
def update_f_all(F,k,delta,n):
    if delta == 0.0: return
    center=n-1
    p = np.arange(n)
    idx1 = center + k - p
    idx2 = center - k + p
    F[p, idx1] -= delta
    F[p, idx2] -= delta
```
Wait we derived add -delta (delta change h). So `F[p, idx] += -delta` == `-= delta`. If delta negative (h_j decrease), -= negative adds. Good.
Potential issue advanced indexing `F[p, idx1] -= delta` with p and idx1 arrays. If idx1 has duplicates? no. If idx1 and idx2 in same call? They are separate calls. If p values repeated? no.

`convex_bounds` as above.

`pair_feasible` and `pair_choose_delta`. Use `size` attributes.

In `pair_feasible`, if c_pos.size and f_pos maybe zeros? excluded. Use `np.min` on empty? size check.

`zero_max` maybe -1e300; comparison fine.

`pair_find_delta`: `for idx in (center, sp1, sp2):` If sp1==sp2 (i=j) but we exclude, still if center? no. If n=2 and i/j, sp1=2,sp2=0 distinct. Good.

`pairwise_optimize`: Need if n<2 return. Code:
```
def pairwise_optimize(h, n, deadline, iters=38, k=None):
    if n < 2:
       h=project_sum(h,0.5*n); return h, compute_c5(h)
    if k is None:
       k = 40 if n <= 500 else 60
    h = project_sum(h, 0.5*n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')[::-1].astype(float)
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
    M = float(c.max()); dx=2.0/n
    best_c = M*dx; best_h = h.copy()
    f_all = build_f_all(h,n)
    center=n-1
    stale=0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(24680)
    for sweep in range(10000):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       pre_M=M
       order=rng.permutation(n)
       act = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:k]
       f_act = f_all[:, act]
       c_act = c[act]
       for i in order:
           if time.time() > deadline: break
           a = float(h[i])
           low = np.maximum(-a, h - 1.0)
           up = np.minimum(1.0 - a, h)
           Fmat = f_all[i, act, None] - f_act
           add = np.where(Fmat > 0.0, Fmat * low[None, :], Fmat * up[None, :])
           scores = (c_act[None, :] + add).max(axis=0) # Wait c_act[None,:] shape 1,K not K,1! Need c_act[:,None] + add.
```
Important: `c_act` length K. add shape K,n. Use `c_act[:, None] + add`. Then max axis=0. Good.
```
           scores[i] = np.inf
           bad = up <= low + 1e-12
           if bad.any(): scores[bad] = np.inf
           j = int(np.argmin(scores))
           if not np.isfinite(scores[j]): continue
           bj = float(h[j])
           Fv = f_all[i] - f_all[j]
           sp1 = center + (j - i)
           sp2 = center + (i - j)
           delta, tout = pair_find_delta(c, Fv, a, bj, center, iters, M, sp1, sp2)
           if abs(delta) > 1e-8:
               nc = c + delta * Fv
               nc[center] -= 2.0 * delta * delta
               nc[sp1] += delta * delta
               nc[sp2] += delta * delta
               if nc.min() < -1e-5:
                   nc = np.clip(nc, 0.0, None)
               nM = float(nc.max())
               if nM <= M + 1e-7:
                   h[i] = a + delta
                   h[j] = bj - delta
                   update_f_all(f_all, i, delta, n)
                   update_f_all(f_all, j, -delta, n)
                   c = nc
                   if nM < M - 1e-12:
                       M = nM
                   if M*dx < best_c:
                       best_c = M*dx
                       best_h = h.copy()
                   act = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:k]
                   f_act = f_all[:, act]
                   c_act = c[act]
       c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')[::-1].astype(float)
       c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
       M = float(c.max())
       f_all = build_f_all(h, n)
       cur = M*dx
       if cur < best_c:
           best_c = cur
           best_h = h.copy()
       if pre_M - M < 1e-8:
           stale += 1
       else:
           stale = 0
       if stale >= 6:
           break
    best_h = project_sum(best_h, 0.5*n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c
```

`sl_grad`: Use `x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)` maybe no copy. If G_N=0? not.

```
def sl_grad(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = G_N
    alpha = G_ALPHA
    q = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, 'full')
    mu = float(q.max())
    sh = alpha * (q - mu)
    sh -= float(sh.max())
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    w = e / s
    g = np.zeros(n)
    center = n - 1
    idxs = np.nonzero(w > 1e-12)[0]
    for p in idxs:
       wk = float(w[p]); d = center - int(p)
       ...
    return g
```
For d<0: e_ = -d; `g[e_:] += wk*(1.0-x[:-e_])`, `g[:n-e_] -= wk*x[e_:]`. Be careful variable e_ vs exp. Use `ell`.
```
       if d > 0:
          m = n - d
          g[:m] += wk * (1.0 - x[d:])
          g[d:] -= wk * x[:m]
       elif d < 0:
          ell = -d
          g[ell:] += wk * (1.0 - x[:-ell])
          g[:n-ell] -= wk * x[ell:]
       else:
          g += wk * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
```
Good.

`slsqp_smooth`: Use bounds list. `constraints = {'type':'eq','fun':sl_cons}`? SciPy accepts dict or list of dicts. Use list.
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    global G_N, G_ALPHA
    G_N = int(n); G_ALPHA = float(alpha)
    target=0.5*n
    h0=project_sum(h,target)
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] * n
    try:
       res = minimize(sl_obj, h0, jac=sl_grad, bounds=bounds,
                      constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':sl_cons}],
                      method='SLSQP', options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12,'disp':False})
       x=res.x
    except Exception:
       x=h0
    return project_sum(x,target)
```
`sl_cons` uses G_N. Good.

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    B=float(budget_s)
    hard=t0+B-2.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0=np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n=int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n=400
        h0=np.full(n,0.5)
    if n < 2:
       return project_sum(h0,0.5*n), compute_c5(h0), n
    if n > 800:
       nd=600
       xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n); xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,nd)
       h0=np.interp(xn,xo,h0)
       n=nd
    h0=project_sum(h0,0.5*n)
    best_h=h0.copy()
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    p1=t0+0.50*B
    h,c=pairwise_optimize(h0,n,min(p1, hard), iters=38, k=40 if n<=500 else 60)
    if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
    p2=t0+0.74*B
    if n <= 550 and time.time() < p2:
       alphas=[4.0, 12.0, 40.0, 160.0, 640.0, 2500.0, 10000.0]
       for al in alphas:
           if time.time() > p2 - 10.0: break
           maxit=100 if al < 500.0 else 50
           hh=slsqp_smooth(best_h,n,al,maxit)
           ch=compute_c5(hh)
           if ch < best_c:
               best_c=ch; best_h=hh.copy()
    p3=t0+0.87*B
    if time.time() < p3:
       h,c=pairwise_optimize(best_h,n,min(p3,hard), iters=42,k=40 if n<=500 else 60)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
    # high res
    if n < 700 and time.time() < hard - 80.0:
       n2 = int(min(700, max(500, round(n*1.5))))
       if n2 != n:
          rem=hard-time.time()
          if rem > 120.0:
             sub = t0 + min(rem-5.0, 0.25*B)
             xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n); xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n2)
             hh=np.interp(xn,xo,best_h)
             hh=project_sum(hh,0.5*n2)
             h2,c2=pairwise_optimize(hh,n2,sub,iters=36,k=50 if n2<=500 else 70)
             if c2 < best_c:
                best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy(); n=n2
    # perturb
    if time.time() < hard - 20.0:
       attempts=[0.002,0.00,0.006,0.012] # maybe 0.0 for 0.5? Use structured separate
       for it, sig in enumerate(attempts):
          rem=hard-time.time()
          if rem < 35.0: break
          if it == 1:
             hh=best_h.copy()
             cnt=max(1, n//25)
             idx=rng.choice(n, size=cnt, replace=False)
             hh[idx]=0.5
             hh=project_sum(hh,0.5*n)
          else:
             hh=best_h + sig*rng.normal(size=n)
             hh=project_sum(hh,0.5*n)
          sub = t0 + min(rem-2.0, max(18.0, 0.03*B))
          h2,c2=pairwise_optimize(hh,n,sub,iters=34,k=40 if n<=500 else 60)
          if c2 < best_c:
             best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy()
    best_h=project_sum(best_h,0.5*n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c, n
```

Write final code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

G_N = 0
G_ALPHA = 1.0

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_sum(x, target):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    target = float(target)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    if abs(float(x.sum()) - target) < 1e-13:
        return x
    lo = float(x.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(x.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if float(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()) > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        m = (y > 1e-9) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-9)
        if m.any():
            y[m] += diff / float(m.sum())
    return np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
...
```
If diff sign and m addition push outside 1, final clip could change sum. But diff tiny. fine.

`convex_bounds`:
```
def convex_bounds(low, high, c, F, t):
    D = F*F - 4.0*(c - t)
    if D < -1e-10:
        return low, high, False
    if D <= 1e-11:
        r = -0.5 * F
        if r < low - 1e-10 or r > high + 1e-10:
            return low, high, False
        return r, r, True
    s = np.sqrt(D)
    r1 = -0.5 * (F + s)
    r2 = -0.5 * (F - s)
    if r1 > low:
        low = float(r1)
    if r2 < high:
        high = float(r2)
    if low > high + 1e-10:
        return low, high, False
    return low, high, True
```

`pair_feasible` and choose use floats.

In `pair_choose_delta`, after applying convex, if low/high maybe point. If center D large roots. Candidate left interval: if low <= r1 but high might be < low? already checked. `hi = min(high, r1)`. If low <= hi+tol: cands. right: `lo = max(low, r2)`; if lo <= high+tol. Good.

`pair_find_delta`: The variable `b` for h_j. Bounds `dl = max(-a, b - 1.0)`, `du = min(1.0 - a, b)`. If a,b floats. For a=0,b=0: dl=max(0,-1)=0, du=min(1,0)=0. return 0. For a=1,b=1: dl=max(-1,0)=0, du=min(0,1)=0. For a=0,b=1: dl=max(0,0)=0, du=min(1,1)=1. good.

`pairwise_optimize`: In inner loop, after `Fmat = f_all[i, act][:, None] - f_act.T`, `f_all[i, act]` shape K? Advanced indexing with act list returns Kx1? Actually `f_all[i, act]` shape (len(act),). Good. Add `[:, None]`. Good.
`add = np.where(Fmat > 0.0, Fmat * low[None, :], Fmat * up[None, :])`. low shape n. Good.
`scores = (c_act[:, None] + add).max(axis=0)`. c_act length K. Good.
After accept, `f_act = f_all[:, act]` can be a view? `f_all[:, act]` advanced indexing returns copy. That's fine. But if we later update f_all, f_act copy stale; we recompute after accept. Within i loop before accept, f_act current. If no accept, unchanged. Good.
`sp1 = center + (j - i)` etc. If sp1 == center? only if j=i, but score inf. Good.

Potential: `j` chosen maybe `i` if `scores[i]=np.inf` but all others larger? inf min not chosen if finite others. Good.

```
   ...

   elif nM < M - ...: M = nM
```

```
   ...
   M = float(nc.max()) if nM > M else M
```
Actually c=nc, so `M = float(nc.max())` after accept. Then best if M*dx < best_c. This handles equal/increase. But accepting increase could unhelpful. Require `nM <= M + 1e-9` (very small). Use 1e-8. If nM > M but within, set M=nM. If improvement, M lower. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

G_N = 0
G_ALPHA = 1.0


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')) * (2.0 / n))


def project_sum(x, target):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    target = float(target)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    if abs(float(x.sum()) - target) < 1e-13:
        return x
    lo = float(x.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(x.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if float(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()) > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        m = (y > 1e-9) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-9)
        if m.any():
            y[m] += diff / float(m.sum())
    return np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)


def build_f_all(h, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    center = n - 1
    F = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=float)
    b = 1.0 - h
    hr = h[::-1]
    for p in range(n):
        F[p, center - p:center - p + n] += b
        F[p, center + p - n + 1:center + p + 1] += -hr
    return F


def update_f_all(F, k, delta, n):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    center = n - 1
    p = np.arange(n)
    idx1 = center + k - p
    idx2 = center - k + p
    F[p, idx1] -= delta
    F[p, idx2] -= delta


def convex_bounds(low, high, c, F, t):
    D = F * F - 4.0 * (c - t)
    if D < -1e-10:
        return low, high, False
    if D <= 1e-11:
        r = -0.5 * F
        if r < low - 1e-10 or r > high + 1e-10:
            return low, high, False
        return float(r), float(r), True
    s = np.sqrt(D)
    r1 = -0.5 * (F + s)
    r2 = -0.5 * (F - s)
    if r1 > low:
        low = float(r1)
    if r2 < high:
        high = float(r2)
    if low > high + 1e-10:
        return low, high, False
    return low, high, True


def pair_feasible(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max,
                  c0, F0, c1, F1, c2, F2, dl, du, t):
    low = dl
    high = du
    if c_pos.size:
        r = float(np.min((t - c_pos) / f_pos))
        if r < high:
            high = r
    if c_neg.size:
        r = float(np.max((t - c_neg) / f_neg))
        if r > low:
            low = r
    if low > high + 1e-9:
        return False
    if zero_max > t + 1e-8:
        return False
    low, high, ok = convex_bounds(low, high, c1, F1, t)
    if not ok:
        return False
    low, high, ok = convex_bounds(low, high, c2, F2, t)
    if not ok:
        return False
    D = F0 * F0 - 8.0 * (t - c0)
    if D >= 0.0:
        s = np.sqrt(D)
        r1 = 0.25 * (F0 - s)
        r2 = 0.25 * (F0 + s)
        if not (high <= r1 + 1e-9 or low >= r2 - 1e-9):
            return False
    return True


def pair_choose_delta(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max,
                      c0, F0, c1, F1, c2, F2, dl, du, t):
    low = dl
    high = du
    if c_pos.size:
        r = float(np.min((t - c_pos) / f_pos))
        if r < high:
            high = r
    if c_neg.size:
        r = float(np.max((t - c_neg) / f_neg))
        if r > low:
            low = r
    if low > high + 1e-9:
        return 0.0
    if zero_max > t + 1e-7:
        return 0.0
    low, high, ok = convex_bounds(low, high, c1, F1, t)
    if not ok:
        return 0.0
    low, high, ok = convex_bounds(low, high, c2, F2, t)
    if not ok:
        return 0.0
    D = F0 * F0 - 8.0 * (t - c0)
    cands = []
    if D >= 0.0:
        s = np.sqrt(D)
        r1 = 0.25 * (F0 - s)
        r2 = 0.25 * (F0 + s)
        if low <= r1 + 1e-10:
            hi = min(high, r1)
            if low <= hi + 1e-10:
                cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0, low, hi)))
        if high >= r2 - 1e-10:
            lo = max(low, r2)
            if lo <= high + 1e-10:
                cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0, lo, high)))
    else:
        cands.append(float(np.clip(0.0, low, high)))
    if not cands:
        return 0.0
    best = float(cands[0])
    best_abs = abs(best)
    for v in cands[1:]:
        av = abs(float(v))
        if av < best_abs - 1e-12:
            best = float(v)
            best_abs = av
    return float(np.clip(best, dl, du))


def pair_find_delta(c, F, a, b, center, iters, hi, sp1, sp2):
    dl = max(-a, b - 1.0)
    du = min(1.0 - a, b)
    if du - dl <= 1e-13:
        return 0.0, float(hi)
    pos = F > 1e-12
    neg = F < -1e-12
    for idx in (center, sp1, sp2):
        pos[idx] = False
        neg[idx] = False
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    for idx in (center, sp1, sp2):
        zero[idx] = False
    c_pos = c[pos]
    f_pos = F[pos]
    c_neg = c[neg]
    f_neg = F[neg]
    zero_max = float(c[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e300
    c0 = float(c[center])
    F0 = float(F[center])
    c1 = float(c[sp1])
    F1 = float(F[sp1])
    c2 = float(c[sp2])
    F2 = float(F[sp2])
    lo = 0.0
    hi = float(hi)
    for _ in range(iters):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if pair_feasible(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max,
                         c0, F0, c1, F1, c2, F2, dl, du, mid):
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    d = pair_choose_delta(c_pos, f_pos, c_neg, f_neg, zero_max,
                          c0, F0, c1, F1, c2, F2, dl, du, hi + 1e-8)
    if d < dl:
        d = dl
    elif d > du:
        d = du
    return float(d), hi


def pairwise_optimize(h, n, deadline, iters=40, k=None):
    if n < 2:
        h = project_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
        return h, compute_c5(h)
    if k is None:
        k = 50 if n <= 450 else 70
    h = project_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')[::-1].astype(float)
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
    M = float(c.max())
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best_c = M * dx
    best_h = h.copy()
    f_all = build_f_all(h, n)
    center = n - 1
    stale = 0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(24680)

    for _ in range(10000):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        pre_M = M
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        act = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:k]
        f_act = f_all[:, act]
        c_act = c[act]

        for i in order:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            a = float(h[i])
            low = np.maximum(-a, h - 1.0)
            up = np.minimum(1.0 - a, h)
            Fmat = f_all[i, act][:, None] - f_act.T
            add = np.where(Fmat > 0.0, Fmat * low[None, :], Fmat * up[None, :])
            scores = (c_act[:, None] + add).max(axis=0)
            scores[int(i)] = np.inf
            bad = up <= low + 1e-12
            if bad.any():
                scores[bad] = np.inf
            j = int(np.argmin(scores))
            if not np.isfinite(scores[j]):
                continue
            bj = float(h[j])
            Fv = f_all[i] - f_all[j]
            sp1 = center + (int(j) - int(i))
            sp2 = center + (int(i) - int(j))
            delta, _ = pair_find_delta(c, Fv, a, bj, center, iters, M, sp1, sp2)
            if abs(delta) > 1e-8:
                nc = c + delta * Fv
                nc[center] -= 2.0 * delta * delta
                nc[sp1] += delta * delta
                nc[sp2] += delta * delta
                if nc.min() < -1e-5:
                    nc = np.clip(nc, 0.0, None)
                nM = float(nc.max())
                if nM <= M + 1e-8:
                    h[i] = a + delta
                    h[j] = bj - delta
                    update_f_all(f_all, int(i), delta, n)
                    update_f_all(f_all, int(j), -delta, n)
                    c = nc
                    M = float(nc.max())
                    if M * dx < best_c:
                        best_c = M * dx
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    act = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:k]
                    f_act = f_all[:, act]
                    c_act = c[act]

        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')[::-1].astype(float)
        c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
        M = float(c.max())
        f_all = build_f_all(h, n)
        cur = M * dx
        if cur < best_c:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()
        if pre_M - M < 1e-8:
            stale += 1
        else:
            stale = 0
        if stale >= 6:
            break

    best_h = project_sum(best_h, 0.5 * n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c


def sl_obj(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    alpha = G_ALPHA
    q = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, 'full')
    mu = float(q.max())
    sh = alpha * (q - mu)
    sh -= float(sh.max())
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return mu
    return float(mu + np.log(s) / alpha)


def sl_grad(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = G_N
    alpha = G_ALPHA
    q = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, 'full')
    mu = float(q.max())
    sh = alpha * (q - mu)
    sh -= float(sh.max())
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    w = e / s
    g = np.zeros(n)
    center = n - 1
    idxs = np.nonzero(w > 1e-12)[0]
    for p in idxs:
        wk = float(w[p])
        d = center - int(p)
        if d > 0:
            m = n - d
            g[:m] += wk * (1.0 - x[d:])
            g[d:] -= wk * x[:m]
        elif d < 0:
            ell = -d
            g[ell:] += wk * (1.0 - x[:-ell])
            g[:n - ell] -= wk * x[ell:]
        else:
            g += wk * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    return g


def sl_cons(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * G_N)


def slsqp_smooth(h, n, alpha, maxiter):
    global G_N, G_ALPHA
    G_N = int(n)
    G_ALPHA = float(alpha)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h0 = project_sum(h, target)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(sl_obj, h0, jac=sl_grad, bounds=bounds,
                       constraints=[{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sl_cons}],
                       method='SLSQP',
                       options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12, 'disp': False})
        x = res.x
    except Exception:
        x = h0
    return project_sum(x, target)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    B = float(budget_s)
    hard = t0 + B - 2.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n = 400
        h0 = np.full(n, 0.5)

    if n < 2:
        h0 = project_sum(h0, 0.5 * n)
        return h0, compute_c5(h0), n

    if n > 800:
        nd = 600
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nd)
        h0 = np.interp(xn, xo, h0)
        n = nd

    h0 = project_sum(h0, 0.5 * n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    p1 = t0 + 0.50 * B
    h, c = pairwise_optimize(h0, n, min(p1, hard), iters=40, k=50 if n <= 450 else 70)
    if c < best_c:
        best_c = c
        best_h = h.copy()

    p2 = t0 + 0.74 * B
    if n <= 550 and time.time() < p2:
        alphas = [4.0, 12.0, 40.0, 160.0, 640.0, 2500.0, 10000.0]
        for al in alphas:
            if time.time() > p2 - 10.0:
                break
            maxit = 100 if al < 500.0 else 50
            hh = slsqp_smooth(best_h, n, al, maxit)
            ch = compute_c5(hh)
            if ch < best_c:
                best_c = ch
                best_h = hh.copy()

    p3 = t0 + 0.87 * B
    if time.time() < p3:
        h, c = pairwise_optimize(best_h, n, min(p3, hard), iters=44, k=50 if n <= 450 else 70)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h.copy()

    if n < 700 and time.time() < hard - 80.0:
        n2 = int(min(700, max(500, round(n * 1.5))))
        if n2 != n:
            rem = hard - time.time()
            if rem > 120.0:
                sub = time.time() + min(rem - 5.0, 0.25 * B)
                xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
                xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n2)
                hh = np.interp(xn, xo, best_h)
                hh = project_sum(hh, 0.5 * n2)
                h2, c2 = pairwise_optimize(hh, n2, sub, iters=38, k=70)
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_c = c2
                    best_h = h2.copy()
                    n = n2

    if time.time() < hard - 25.0:
        for mode in range(4):
            rem = hard - time.time()
            if rem < 35.0:
                break
            if mode == 1:
                hh = best_h.copy()
                cnt = max(1, n // 25)
                idx = rng.choice(n, size=cnt, replace=False)
                hh[idx] = 0.5
                hh = project_sum(hh, 0.5 * n)
            else:
                sig = [0.002, 0.005, 0.010, 0.018][mode]
                hh = best_h + sig * rng.normal(size=n)
                hh = project_sum(hh, 0.5 * n)
            sub = time.time() + min(rem - 2.0, max(18.0, 0.035 * B))
            h2, c2 = pairwise_optimize(hh, n, sub, iters=36, k=50 if n <= 450 else 70)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()

    best_h = project_sum(best_h, 0.5 * n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c, n
```
